TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于token级"存在系数"（active-presence coefficient）的新型注意力机制OAttention，将每个token的隐藏载体范数映射为0到1之间的存在度，同时用于门控发射信息和控制参与共享计算的权重，从而实现精确的"空token"语义（零向量为零元素），并配套设计局部组件（OFFN、ONorm等）、O-Closure定律及完整的OTransformer架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统注意力掩码是关系级别的控制，只能指定query-source对之间是否可以交互，但无法提供一种由表示携带的、在注意力边界处"不参与"的token状态。这使得在处理缺失值、空token等场景时缺乏精确的代数和组合语义。

Method: 为每个token的隐藏载体h_i定义存在系数p_i=||h_i||²/(τ+||h_i||²)，该系数双重生效：门控token i发射的信息，以及决定token i与其他token共享计算的权重。OAttention在注意力分子和分母中对源token j均用p_j加权，保留标准得分、可见性关系、指数竞争和值聚合。在此基础上构建O-Closure定律M(H⊕0)=M(H)⊕0、局部O-components及OTransformer。

Result: 通过契约测试和GPU评估验证规范算子。在零微调改造的预训练TabPFN v3回归器中，校准版OAttention和Full-O变体在18个匹配数据集-种子案例上平均RMSE变化分别为+0.088%和+0.177%。双块消融实验表明单独OAttention无法在普通宿主组件中保持NULL状态，而完整OTransformer路径可以。

Conclusion: OAttention及OTransformer在精确性、活跃路径兼容性和组合必要性方面通过针对性测试，但作者明确限定范围：不声称普遍无损、任意宿主闭包、学习到的原点吸引或缺失值的通用语义。

Abstract: Attention masks are relation-level controls: they specify which query--source pairs may interact. They do not provide a representation-carried token state that is non-participating at the attention boundary. We assign each token hidden carrier \(h_i\) an active-presence coefficient \(p_i=\lVert h_i\rVert^2/(τ+\lVert h_i\rVert^2)\). The same coefficient has two roles: it gates information emitted by token \(i\), and it determines the mass with which token \(i\) enters computations shared with other tokens.
  OAttention is the support-coupled attention realization of this rule. It gates the receiver output by \(p_i\) and weights source \(j\) by \(p_j\) in both the attention numerator and partition, while retaining the standard score, visibility relation, exponential competition, and value aggregation. This makes the zero-vector token a zero element and yields exact null-receiver, null-source insertion, self-attention insertion, and empty-support properties. The same token-level presence gives local O-components (OFFN, ONorm, and OInject), presence-weighted OStandardize, the O-Closure law \(M(H\oplus0)=M(H)\oplus0\), and an OTransformer by residual and compositional closure.
  The canonical operator is checked by contract tests and a GPU evaluation. In a zero-fine-tuning retrofit of a cloned pretrained TabPFN v3 regressor, calibrated hidden-carrier OAttention and Full-O variants change mean RMSE by $+0.088\%$ and $+0.177\%$, respectively, over 18 matched dataset--seed cases. A two-block ablation shows that OAttention alone does not preserve a NULL state through ordinary host components, whereas the OTransformer path does. These are scoped tests of exactness, active-path compatibility, and compositional necessity; they do not establish universal no-loss, arbitrary-host closure, learned attraction to the origin, or a general semantics for missing values.

</details>


### [232] [SENTRY: Deterministic, Intelligent Risk Assessment for IT Change Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21203)
*Daniel Arulpragasam,Christer Henrysson,Ella Ly,Deepika Anbalagan,Leo Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SENTRY是一个用确定性机器学习流水线（XGBoost + 混合RAG）替代问卷式评分的技术变更风险评估平台，在大型金融机构中取得ROC AUC 0.87和85%准确率，高风险变更检测率约为现有流程的3.25倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型金融机构的技术变更管理依赖风险评估，但现有自报问卷方式主观性强、易被操纵，且难以区分常规变更与后续引发重大事故的变更，迫切需要更准确、一致且可审计的评估方法。

Method: SENTRY采用确定性机器学习流水线：使用XGBoost梯度提升决策树作为核心模型，搭配混合语义与词法检索的RAG机制。系统整合结构化运营元数据、应用依赖图和历史事故记录，通过混合检索从历史变更请求中提取非结构化文本中的风险信号，并将该信号压缩为单个标量特征后输入模型推理，同时通过SHAP值保持逐预测可解释性。

Result: 在企业级规模变更数据上，SENTRY达到ROC AUC 0.87和整体准确率85%，高风险变更的检测率约为现有流程的3.25倍。

Conclusion: 作者最后审视了该设计背后的架构权衡及其对受监管环境中使用机器学习的启示，表明确定性设计与可解释性在受监管变更管理中的重要性。

Abstract: Technology change management in large financial institutions depends on risk assessments that are accurate, consistent, and auditable. In practice, many institutions still rely on self-reported questionnaires. Those questionnaires are subjective, easy to game, and poor at separating routine changes from the ones that later trigger major incidents. This paper presents SENTRY, a risk assessment platform that replaces questionnaire-based scoring with a deterministic machine learning pipeline built from gradient-boosted decision trees (XGBoost) and hybrid retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). The system combines structured operational metadata, application dependency graphs, and historical incident records with a hybrid semantic and lexical search over historical change requests. The retrieval step captures the risk signal in unstructured change request text, then compresses that signal into a single scalar feature before model inference. That design keeps the model deterministic and preserves per-prediction explainability via SHAP values. Evaluated on enterprise-scale change data, SENTRY achieves a ROC AUC of 0.87 and 85% overall accuracy, and it detects high-risk changes at roughly 3.25 times the rate of the existing process. We close by examining the architectural trade-offs behind this design and what they imply for the use of machine learning in regulated change management.

</details>


### [233] [Personalized Privacy Control in LLMs via Attention Head Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21209)
*Junseok Kim,Nakyeong Yang,Kyomin Jung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出个性化隐私的概念，将用户特定的披露偏好纳入隐私控制，并构建了P3Bench基准来扩展上下文隐私策略。研究发现基于提示的策略难以可靠执行个性化隐私策略，为此提出Repair方法——一种推理时注意力头干预技术，显著提升模型对用户特定隐私偏好的遵循度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理式AI使LLM能够访问多样化的用户数据，引发严重隐私问题。现有上下文隐私研究关注模型是否依据情境规范调节信息披露，但忽略了即使在相同情境下，不同用户的可接受披露边界也可能不同。这一局限性促使作者引入个性化隐私概念。

Method: 1）提出个性化隐私（personalized privacy）概念，将用户特定披露偏好融入隐私控制；2）构建P3Bench基准，在上下文隐私策略基础上扩展个性化披露策略；3）提出Repair方法，一种推理时的注意力头干预技术，通过调整披露行为使其趋于策略一致的回答。

Result: 实验表明基于提示的策略无法可靠执行个性化隐私策略，Qwen2.5-7B和Gemma3-4B的平均策略忽视率分别高达51.25%和74.28%。Repair方法显著改善了模型对用户特定隐私偏好的遵循度，减少了模型无法遵循给定策略的情况。

Conclusion: 个性化隐私是超越上下文隐私的重要方向，Repair方法提供了一种有效的推理时干预手段来增强LLM对个性化隐私策略的遵循能力，为代理式AI场景下的隐私保护提供了新思路。

Abstract: The rise of agentic AI enables LLMs to access diverse user data, raising critical privacy concerns. Prior work on contextual privacy studies whether LLMs regulate information disclosure according to context-dependent norms. However, acceptable disclosure boundaries may vary across users even within the same context. To address this limitation, we introduce \textit{personalized privacy}, which incorporates user-specific disclosure preferences into privacy control. We further present P3Bench~(\textbf{P}ersonalized \textbf{P}rivacy \textbf{P}reservation \textbf{Bench}mark), a novel benchmark extending contextual privacy policies with personalized disclosure policies. Experiments show that prompt-based policies fail to reliably enforce personalized privacy policies, with Qwen2.5-7B and Gemma3-4B showing average policy ignorance ratios of 51.25\% and 74.28\%, respectively. Finally, to address this problem, we propose \textsc{Repair}, a robust inference-time attention head intervention method that adjusts disclosure behavior toward policy-consistent responses. Our method significantly improves adherence to user-specific privacy preferences by reducing cases where the model fails to follow the given policy.

</details>


### [234] [Enhancing LLMs in Predictive Political QA with Semi-Structured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21218)
*Yinan Liu,Zihan Zhou,Zichun Jin,Xinyu Wang,Bin Wang,Xiaochun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PSL框架，通过双视图（语义立场景与向量结构场）从半结构化政治记录中提取推理导向证据，提升预测性政治问答中LLM的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测性政治问答（如预测政治人物投票行为）超越了事实查询。现有外部政治资源包含丰富历史证据但很少直接包含答案；现有LLM增强方法（如演员画像模拟、知识图谱证据注入）将外部资源主要视为基于知识的证据，未能充分建模预测相关信号。

Method: 提出PSL（双视图框架），识别两种互补信号：1) 语义视图——从问题相关演员记录中提取立场（stance）信号，捕捉议题特定的偏好；2) 向量视图——从演员交互图中学习结构感知的演员表示，捕捉政治演员间的间接依赖（高阶结构）信号。将两种信号转化为面向推理的证据注入LLM。

Result: 在三个真实数据集和多种LLM上，PSL一致优于基线方法；消融实验证实立场信号与结构信号具有互补增益。

Conclusion: 立场信号与高阶结构信号是预测性政治问答中互补且有效的两种推理信号，PSL双视图框架能有效利用半结构化政治记录提升LLM的预测推理能力。

Abstract: Predictive political question answering (QA), such as predicting how a political actor will vote, goes beyond factual lookup. External political resources offer rich historical evidence, but rarely contain the answer itself. Existing LLM augmentation methods, including actor-profile-based simulation and knowledge graph evidence injection, improve political reasoning but largely treat external resources as knowledge-based evidence, leaving prediction-relevant signals under-modeled. We identify two complementary signals for predictive political QA: actor stances that capture issue-specific preferences, and high-order structure signals that capture indirect dependencies among political actors. We propose PSL, a dual-view framework that converts semi-structured political records into inference-oriented evidence for LLMs. PSL extracts stance signals from question-relevant actor records in a semantic view, and learns structure-aware actor representations from an actor interaction graph in a vector view. Across three real-world datasets and multiple LLMs, PSL consistently outperforms baselines, with ablations confirming the complementary gains of stance and structure signals.

</details>


### [235] [Ontology-supported AI Model and Dataset Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21224)
*Jan Novacek,Ali Ahari,Tobias Müller,Sebastian Reiter,Alexander Viehl,Oliver Bringmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个AI模型交换平台，该平台通过引入本体论来管理、交换和分析AI模型及数据集，旨在解决工业环境中AI资产管理和语义鸿沟问题，并通过实时关键系统的用例验证其效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同组织在AI研究和应用过程中会产生和使用大量资产（模型、数据集等），需要对其进行追踪、追溯和管理；同时，如何有效发现与当前任务相关的资产以及在工业环境中无语义鸿沟地交换和管理AI模型及相关资产，是一个尚待解决的关键问题。

Method: 提出一个AI模型交换平台，该平台集成本体论（ontology），以促进对AI模型与数据集交换、管理和分析所需要素的共同理解，从而解决语义不一致和资产管理问题。

Result: 通过一个实时关键系统的用例演示，阐明了该平台在促进AI模型及数据集的使用、交换和分析方面的实际效用。

Conclusion: 该平台为工业环境中AI模型及相关资产的有效交换与管理提供了可行方案，本体论的引入有助于消除语义鸿沟，并提升AI资产的可追溯性和可发现性。

Abstract: Recently, there has been a great deal of research into improving AI methods and their application. The main focus is on tracking progress, enabling transparent comparisons, and fostering a more profound understanding of AI. In that process, different organizations generate and use plenty of assets that need to be tracked, traced and managed. Moreover, it is important to discover assets relevant for the task at hand. This paper presents research aiming to contribute to answering the question of what is required to exchange and manage AI models and related assets effectively without semantic gaps in an industrial context. We introduce a platform for AI model exchange, which facilitates the usage, exchange, and analysis of AI models and datasets. The platform incorporates an ontology that can foster a more profound common understanding of what is required in these tasks and help tackle the issues mentioned above. Finally, we elucidate the utility of the platform through the illustration of a use case in the context of real-time critical systems.

</details>


### [236] [Fine-Grain GPU Parallelization of the Generalized Partition Crossover for Large-Scale Traveling Salesman Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21233)
*Swetha Varadarajan,Darrell Whitley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于GPU的细粒度并行实现方法，用于大规模TSP问题中广义划分交叉（GPX）算子的分区阶段。通过将分区问题重构为图并行问题，利用CUDA实现并行化，在10,000到200万城市规模的实例上实现了48x-625x的加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于遗传算法（特别是EAX算子）的TSP求解器虽在基准实例上达到最先进性能，但其交叉操作涉及不规则内存访问、图遍历和顺序依赖，限制了在超大规模并行架构上的可扩展性。现有GPU求解器主要利用种群级并行，仅适用于小规模问题，缺乏对算子级并行（尤其是GPX分区阶段）的细粒度并行化研究。

Method: 将GPX分区阶段重新表述为图并行问题，采用合并内存布局（coalesced memory layouts）、幽灵节点变换（ghost-node transformations）和连通分量分析（connected-component analysis）三大关键技术。具体并行化了四个步骤：父代环路的并集构造、四度顶点分裂、公共边删除及重组分量识别，全部通过CUDA实现。

Result: 在10,000到200万城市规模的实例上，相比朴素顺序CPU实现，GPU分区实现获得了48x到625x的加速比，同时显著降低了内存开销。

Conclusion: 算子级并行能够显著提升基于遗传算法的TSP求解器在现代众核架构上的可扩展性，为大规模TSP问题的GPU加速提供了新思路。

Abstract: The Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) is one of the most extensively studied NP-hard optimization problems. Genetic Algorithm (GA)-based solvers, such as the Edge Assembly Crossover (EAX), achieve state-of-the-art performance on many benchmark instances. However, the scalability of these approaches in massively parallel architectures remains limited because crossover operations involve irregular memory access patterns, graph traversals, and sequential dependencies. Existing GPU-based TSP solvers primarily exploit population-level parallelism and are limited to relatively small problem sizes. This work presents a fine-grain GPU implementation of the partition phase of the Generalized Partition Crossover (GPX) operator for large-scale TSP instances. The proposed approach reformulates GPX partitioning as a graph-parallel problem using coalesced memory layouts, ghost-node transformations, and connected-component analysis. The im- plementation parallelizes the union of parent tours, the splitting of degree- four vertices, the deletion of common edges, and the identification of recombining components using CUDA. Experimental results on instances ranging from 10,000 to 2 million cities demonstrate substantial acceleration over a naive sequential CPU imple- mentation. The proposed GPU partitioning achieves speedups between 48x and 625x while significantly reducing memory overhead. The re- sults demonstrate that operator-level parallelism can substantially im- prove the scalability of GA-based TSP solvers on modern many-core architectures.

</details>


### [237] [CLEAR: Continuous Latent Adapter Routing for Utility-Preserving LLM Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21278)
*Chengxiao Wang,Enyi Jiang,Xiaojing Liao,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CLEAR框架，通过轻量级隐状态门控连续控制安全低秩适配器的激活强度，在提升LLM安全性的同时最小化对正常输入的效用损失，在Llama-3-8B-Instruct上将HarmBench ASR从32.3%降至0.5%，同时保留大部分基础模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局安全性微调（如SFT或标准LoRA）会同时影响模型对有害和良性输入的响应，导致安全性与效用之间的权衡失衡。现有方法缺乏对安全适配器激活强度的细粒度、连续控制能力。

Method: CLEAR采用轻量级隐藏状态门控机制，连续控制安全低秩适配器的激活强度，冻结基础骨干网络，通过条件性路由实现对有害输入的针对性抑制，同时避免对良性提示产生不必要的扰动。

Result: 在HarmBench上显著提升鲁棒性，Llama-3-8B-Instruct的ASR从32.3%降至0.5%；相比全局SFT或LoRA，GSM8K准确率最高提升7.1个百分点，显著减少效用退化。

Conclusion: CLEAR提供了一种有效的安全-效用权衡机制，通过条件性、连续性的适配器路由控制为LLM对齐提供了新思路，在不牺牲良性输入性能的前提下大幅提升安全性。

Abstract: Improving the safety of large language models (LLMs) often comes at the expense of utility, as globally applied safety tuning may affect model responses to both harmful and benign inputs. We propose \textbf{C}ontinuous \textbf{L}at\textbf{E}nt \textbf{A}dapter \textbf{R}outing (CLEAR), a conditional safety adaptation framework that uses a lightweight hidden-state gate to continuously control the activation strength of a safety low-rank adapter. CLEAR aims to reduce harmful completions while avoiding unnecessary changes to the frozen backbone that could degrade performance on benign prompts. Experiments on widely used safety and utility benchmarks show that CLEAR improves robustness on HarmBench while reducing the utility degradation observed with globally applied safety tuning such as SFT or standard low-rank adaptation (LoRA). On Llama-3-8B-Instruct, CLEAR reduces HarmBench ASR from 32.3\% to 0.5\%, while retaining most of the base model's utility and achieving up to 7.1 percentage points higher GSM8K accuracy than globally applied SFT or LoRA. These results suggest that CLEAR is a promising mechanism for improving the safety--utility trade-off in LLM alignment.

</details>


### [238] [AUSO: Action-Level Unified Skill Optimization from Internalization to Utilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21292)
*Huizu Lin,Chengkai Huang,Tianqi Gao,Tao Huang,Daijiao Liu,Tongxin Li,Xiaoyan Sun,Lina Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AUSO（Action-level Unified Skill Optimization），一种统一的技能学习方法，通过渐进式、动作感知的优化流程，让技能随着策略演化自然过渡——从最初的学习知识来源，到中期支持能力形成，再到最终仅在有益于具体动作时被调用。在ALFWorld、WebShop和SearchQA上的实验表明AUSO一致地提升了智能体性能和分布外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法没有显式建模技能在智能体策略演化过程中的生命周期：要么将技能保留在模型之外，要么完全内化，要么通过有噪声的任务级成功率的启发式在技能内化与利用目标之间选择。这些设计将训练过程碎片化，并且对同一轨迹内的动作赋予统一的重要性，忽略了技能指导可能对某些决策有帮助而对其他决策有干扰的问题。

Method: AUSO通过渐进式、动作感知的优化过程统一技能学习与技能使用。训练初期，联合学习教师引导和环境结果，在保留任务导向反馈的同时获取基础技能；随后强调基于结果的策略优化以巩固自主解决问题的能力；策略成熟后，在每个采样动作的技能条件与技能无关两种上下文中进行评估，将动作级信息信号与轨迹结果优势耦合，使有益于技能敏感的动作获得更强更新，有害动作被抑制。强化学习在所有阶段作为共享骨干。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和SearchQA三个基准上的实验表明，AUSO在智能体性能和分布外泛化方面一致优于具有竞争力的基线方法。

Conclusion: 技能应从外部监督来源渐进过渡为决策知识，其利用应根据动作级别的收益自适应调整；AUSO通过统一的渐进式优化框架实现了这一目标，且以强化学习作为全阶段共享骨干。

Abstract: Skills play different roles as an agent's policy evolves: they should first provide learnable knowledge, then support capability formation, and finally be invoked only when they improve individual decisions. Existing methods rarely model this lifecycle. They either keep skills outside the model, fully internalize them, or select among internalization and utilization objectives through noisy task-level success rates. Such designs fragment training and assign uniform importance to actions within the same trajectory, even though skill guidance may help some decisions while distracting others. To solve these problems, we introduce AUSO (Action-level Unified Skill Optimization), which unifies skill learning and skill use through a progressive, action-aware optimization process. At the beginning of training, AUSO jointly learns from teacher guidance and environmental outcomes, enabling the policy to acquire foundational skills without losing task-oriented feedback. It subsequently emphasizes outcome-based policy optimization to consolidate autonomous problem-solving ability. As the policy matures, AUSO evaluates each sampled action under both skill-conditioned and skill-free contexts. The resulting action-level information signal is coupled with the trajectory outcome advantage, allowing beneficial skill-sensitive actions to receive stronger updates and harmful ones to be suppressed. Therefore, skills gradually transition from an external source of supervision into decision knowledge whose utilization is adapted to its action-level benefit, while reinforcement learning remains the shared backbone across all stages. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and SearchQA show that AUSO consistently improves agent performance and out-of-distribution generalization over competitive baselines.

</details>


### [239] [From Regulation to Implementation: A Critical Evaluation of LLM-Assisted Regulatory Compliance in Industry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21317)
*Adriana Watson,Marco Bücheler,Grant Richards*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了数据提取指令和法规模糊性如何影响LLM生成合规文档（如DPP和DPIA）的质量与一致性，发现指南越宽松（如DPIA）越需要更高上下文的提示来保持一致性和完整性，而指南越严格（如DBP）则输出更一致但更容易产生幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟的ESPR法规引入数字产品护照（DPP），GDPR要求数据保护影响评估（DPIA），而创建这些合规文档非常困难：工业数据格式异构、分散在不同系统中难以提取；DPIA文档缺乏标准格式且需要跨学科专业知识。虽已有研究提出用LLM生成这些文档，但数据提取指令和法规模糊性对LLM输出的影响尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 本文通过探索数据提取指令和法规模糊性对LLM生成合规文档质量与一致性的影响来弥补研究空白。将生成的合规文档与人工创建的地面真值模式进行对比基准测试，评估不同模型在不同指导严格程度下的表现。

Result: 研究结果显示：对指南较为宽松的合规文档（如DPIA格式化），需要更高上下文的提示才能保持输出的一致性和完整性；而对于指南较为严格的文档（如数字电池护照DBP的格式化），无论提示上下文如何，输出一致性都较好，但可能导致更多的幻觉输出。

Conclusion: 研究表明法规指南的严格程度显著影响LLM生成合规文档的性能：宽松指南需要更丰富的上下文来保证质量，而严格指南虽能保证一致性但存在幻觉风险。这为设计LLM辅助合规文档生成系统提供了重要指导。

Abstract: The European Union (EU) has emerged as a leading regulatory body in the development of sustainability and privacy regulations. While new regulation requirements vary, many include a documentation artifact to ensure compliance. Notably, the Ecodesign for Sustainable Products Regulation (ESPR) introduces Digital Product Passports (DPPs) for life cycle transparency, while the General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR) mandates Data Protection Impact Assessments (DPIAs) to mitigate privacy risks. Creating these compliance artifacts, however, is challenging. Industrial data, which often exists in heterogeneous formats and is scattered across company and supplier systems, is required for DPPs and can be difficult to extract into compliant DPP formatting. Furthermore, DPIA documents require interdisciplinary expertise and follow no standardized format, making development difficult for novel systems. To address the particular complexity of compliance artifact creation for both regulations, researchers have proposed the use of LLMs in the generation process; however, the impact of the aforementioned problems on the output of these systems is largely unaddressed. This work investigates the existing research gap by exploring how data extraction instructions and regulatory vagueness impact the quality and consistency of LLM-produced compliance artifacts. The resulting artifacts are evaluated by benchmarking different models against manually created ground-truth schemas. The results reveal that less strict guidelines, such as DPIA formatting, require higher context prompts to maintain consistency and completeness. Stricter guidelines, such as formatting for Digital Battery Passports (DBP), result in consistent results regardless of prompt context, but may lead to more hallucinations in the output

</details>


### [240] [VIALS: A Benchmark for Visual Interpretation of Artifacts in the Life Sciences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21357)
*Elaine Lau,Thanuka Udumulla,Lee Izhaki-Tavor,Francisco Guzmán,Nicholas Magazine,Jonas Mueller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VIALS基准，包含161个源自生物技术产业实验流程（而非出版物中精美图片）的科学图像视觉问答任务，发现前沿视觉语言模型在这些任务上表现不佳，而领域专家却认为这些任务很直接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业生命科学工作流中科学家频繁解读各类科学图像（凝胶电泳、显微图像、质粒图谱、流式细胞图、分子结构等）来指导研究决策，但现有视觉语言模型虽能流利描述自然图像，却无法准确解读这些科学图像，这限制了AI在专业生命科学工作流中的实用价值。

Method: 构建VIALS基准，收集161个视觉问答任务，覆盖生物技术产业实验工作流中出现的各类科学图像类型（非出版物或教材中的精修图像），并用于评估前沿视觉语言模型的科学图像解读能力。

Result: 发现前沿视觉语言模型虽然能流畅描述自然图像，但在解读这些科学图像方面表现不佳，反映出领域知识和领域特定视觉推理能力的不足；而具备相关领域专长的科学家则认为这些视觉解读任务非常简单直接。

Conclusion: 如果AI无法像科学家那样准确解读这些实验工作流中的科学图像，那么它在专业生命科学工作流中的实用价值将非常有限，因为这些图像是科学家推理、交流和决策的核心依据。

Abstract: In professional life sciences workflows, scientists routinely interpret visual artifacts (gel blots, microscopy images, plasmid maps, flow cytometry plots, molecular structures, ...) to inform research decisions. We introduce VIALS, a visual question-answering benchmark with 161 such interpretation tasks, spanning the types of artifacts examined throughout experimental workflows in the biotech industry (rather than polished figures from publications and textbooks). While frontier vision-language models can now fluently describe natural images, we find that they are unable to accurately interpret these scientific images, reflecting limitations in domain knowledge and domain-specific visual reasoning capabilities. In contrast, scientists with relevant domain expertise find these visual interpretation tasks straightforward. AI that cannot similarly interpret such images will have limited utility in professional life sciences workflows, where such artifacts are central to how scientists reason, communicate, and make decisions.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [241] [Humanoid Musical Robots as Experimental Interfaces for Music-Evoked Emotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20433)
*Vincent K. M. Cheung,Jia-Yeu Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将音乐人形机器人作为研究音乐诱发情绪的受控实验平台，指出当前研究受限于预录听觉刺激范式，无法捕捉真实音乐体验的多模态、具身和交互特性，并通过WAS-5萨克斯机器人的案例研究验证了该视角的技术可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音乐情绪研究过度依赖预录音频刺激，这种简化范式无法反映现实音乐体验的多模态、具身化和交互性本质；音乐人形机器人此前主要用于表演和人机交互研究，其作为实验工具探索音乐诱发情绪的潜力尚未被发掘。

Method: 作者采用立场论文（position paper）方法：首先基于音乐认知与情绪理论，识别需要同时操控声学与非声学变量的研究机制；随后论证人形机器人因具备参数化演奏控制、跨试验可重复性以及可解耦重组听觉、视觉和交互成分的能力而适合作为实验界面；最后通过WAS-5萨克斯机器人案例研究验证其技术可行性。

Result: WAS-5案例研究表明，音乐人形机器人能够实现对声学变量和交互变量的可重复控制，满足未来音乐情绪实验的前提条件，从而证明了该研究视角的技术可行性。

Conclusion: 音乐人形机器人可以作为方法论平台，支撑未来对音乐诱发情绪进行受控实验研究，为弥合实验室研究与真实音乐体验之间的差距提供了新的路径。

Abstract: Advances in technology have led to increasingly sophisticated musical humanoid robots. However, their use has largely been limited to performance and related research in human-robot interaction. In this position paper, we propose a novel perspective: musical humanoid robots as experimental interfaces for investigating music-evoked emotions. We argue that current research is constrained by paradigms relying on pre-recorded auditory stimuli, which fail to capture the multimodal, embodied, and interactive nature of real-world musical experience. Building on existing theories of music cognition and emotion, we identify mechanisms that require controlled manipulation of both acoustic and non-acoustic variables. We show that humanoid robots are well-suited as they enable parametric control of performance variables, reproducibility across trials, and the decoupling and recombination of auditory, visual, and interactive components. We illustrate the technical feasibility of this perspective through a case study of the WAseda Saxophonist Robot 5 (WAS-5), demonstrating reproducible control of acoustic and interaction variables that are prerequisites for future music-emotion experiments. Our work positions musical humanoid robots as a methodological platform that enables future controlled investigations of music-evoked emotions.

</details>


### [242] [EndoLIFT: Language-Disambiguated Latent-Conditioned Rectified Flow for Bidirectional Endoscopic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20478)
*Chi Kit Ng,Yidong Zhang,Lui Siu Hing,Jinsong Lin,Tianchun Wu,Ho Yin Chim,Zhiqing Tang,Tao Yang,Huxin Gao,Trevor Yeung,Raymond Shing-Yan Tang,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EndoLIFT，一个面向双向内窥镜控制的视觉-语言-动作策略，通过结合语言指令意图条件化和基于轨迹潜变量的整流流动作专家，解决了内窥镜控制中的"意图混叠"（intent aliasing）问题，即在视觉场景相同但操作阶段不同时要求相反轴向动作的歧义性。实验表明语言选择轴向模式，轨迹潜变量提升方向准确性和鲁棒回缩能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规胃肠内窥镜检查本质上具有双向性——器械需要推进到达目标解剖位置，随后撤回或翻转进行检查，外部提示也可能要求提前反向操作。当要求的操作阶段在视觉场景变化之前发生改变时，几乎相同的视觉观察可能需要相反的轴向动作。这种歧义性被形式化为"意图混叠"（intent aliasing），该问题此前未被系统识别和解决。

Method: EndoLIFT由两部分组成：显式语言指令意图条件化和潜变量条件化的整流流动作专家。策略输入包括RGB图像、语言指令和前序动作状态；一个32维变分轨迹潜变量（VTL）随机地条件化连续动作块生成。该设计将语言选择的轴向模式与轨迹潜变量对方向准确性的贡献分离开来。

Result: 与无潜变量条件的匹配模型相比，EndoLIFT的导航方向准确率提升11.1个百分点，错误方向推进减少83%。架构控制的1位模式标志参考模型表现出较弱的锚点切换能力，而EndoLIFT在44种未见的语言变体上保持82.8%的意图跟随准确率。在闭环评估中，EndoLIFT在已见的结肠模型和未见的肺、胃模型上相较无VTL版本整体成功率提升30个百分点，并在10/10次离体猪气管试验中全部成功。

Conclusion: 实验结果将基于语言的意图选择与轨迹潜变量对方向正确性和鲁棒回缩的贡献成功分离，证明了语言条件化负责轴向模式选择，而轨迹潜变量增强方向准确性和回缩鲁棒性，两者协同实现可靠的双向内窥镜导航。

Abstract: Routine gastrointestinal endoscopy is intrinsically bidirectional: the instrument is advanced to reach target anatomy and later withdrawn or retroflexed for inspection, while an external cue may require earlier reversal. When the requested phase changes before the visual scene does, nearly identical observations can require opposite axial actions. We identify and formalize this ambiguity in bidirectional endoscopic control as intent aliasing. We propose EndoLIFT (Endoscopic Language-Instruction Flow with Trajectory Latents), a vision-language-action policy that combines explicit language-based intent conditioning with a latent-conditioned rectified-flow action expert. The policy receives RGB, a language instruction, and the previous-action state; a 32-D variational trajectory latent stochastically conditions continuous action-chunk generation. Controlled same-observation instruction swaps establish that language selects the axial mode, independently of whether the trajectory latent is present. Relative to the matched model without latent conditioning, EndoLIFT improves navigation-direction accuracy by 11.1 percentage points and reduces wrong-direction advance by 83\%. An architecture-controlled 1-bit mode-flag reference exhibits weaker canonical-anchor switching, while EndoLIFT retains 82.8\% intent-following accuracy across 44 held-out linguistic variants. In closed-loop evaluation, EndoLIFT improves overall success by 30 percentage points over EndoLIFT w/o VTL on both the seen colon phantom and the unseen lung and stomach phantoms, and completes 10/10 ex-vivo porcine-trachea trials. These results separate language-based intent selection from the trajectory latent's contribution to directional correctness and robust retraction.

</details>


### [243] [Koala Gripper: Co-designing Robotic Grippers and Data-Capture Devices for Scaling Dexterous Manipulation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20546)
*Amar Hajj-Ahmad,Zubin Kremer Guha,Tim Fofonoff,Zhi Ern Teoh,Ciarán T. O'Neill,Ben Thacher,Igor Fala,Vidullan Surendran,Murphy Wonsick,Peter Whitney,David Watkins*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个共设计框架，同时开发数据采集设备和机器人执行装置，并在此基础上推出了Koala Gripper系统——一种兼具人机工效与机械灵活性的手持式数据采集夹爪和机器人夹爪平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型操作数据集需求增长，手持式机器人夹爪数据采集及其夹爪设计变得愈发重要。然而现有的数据采集设备设计趋向于匹配已有机器人夹爪的形态，牺牲了人机工效和操作性能。因此需要一种能同时兼顾数据采集和机器人执行两方面约束的设计方法。

Method: 提出一种共设计框架，将数据采集设备与机器人执行装置的平台约束同时纳入设计流程。设计了Koala Gripper系统，包括一个力优化的手指/扳机连杆机构（具有定向反射质量特性）、独特的单体双拇指结构，以及以用户为中心的人体工学设计。其驱动手指具有反向可驱动性，有效质量约为几十克级别。

Result: 该夹爪能够在广泛物体上实现安全抓取、有力的工具使用和精确的分离操作。通过端到端的数据采集与策略执行流水线部署验证，展示了其通过模仿学习实现的能力。

Conclusion: Koala Gripper在保持可扩展性和易用性的同时，相比平行颚式夹爪提升了灵活性和抓取能力，为手持式数据采集与机器人执行装置的协同设计提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: As the demand for larger manipulation datasets grows, handheld robotic gripper data collection and the associated gripper designs become more vital. Current data collection device designs trend towards matching the morphologies of existing robotic grippers, sacrificing ergonomics and manipulation performance. In this paper, we propose a co-design framework that guides the simultaneous development of both data collection and robotic execution devices by weaving both platform constraints into the design process. Through this workflow, we present the Koala Gripper system, a data capture device and robotic gripper platform that improves dexterity and grasp capability compared to parallel jaw grippers while preserving scalability and ease-of-use. The design introduces a novel force-optimized finger/trigger linkage mechanism with directional reflected mass characteristics, a unique monolithic dual-thumb, and user-centered ergonomic design. The design's actuated robotic fingers are backdrivable, with effective mass on the order of tens of grams. We show that these grippers are capable of secure grasps over a wide range of objects, forceful tool use, and precise singulation. We further validate the platform by deploying it with an end-to-end data collection and policy execution pipeline that highlights its capabilities through learning from demonstration. More information available at http://koalagripper.rai-inst.com

</details>


### [244] [Logic-VLA: A Temporal Logic Conditioned Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20556)
*Celina Shiyu Wang,Yiqi Zhao,Junjie Ye,Yue Wang,Jyotirmoy V. Deshmukh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Logic-VLA，一种可感知形式化需求的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，它以推理时提供的信号时序逻辑（STL）规格为条件，通过语法图编码器、STL条件监督微调和基于流匹配的轨迹级偏好优化，在保持自然语言任务性能的同时显著提升形式化需求满足率（提升24.8~40.7个百分点，NL任务成功率最多下降1.8个百分点），并在四旋翼导航仿真中验证了对未见STL公式的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型仅依赖自然语言指令进行任务执行，但自然语言无法精确表达安全关键或时空约束需求（如时序、空间限制）。这导致在安全敏感场景中，VLA可能无法严格满足形式化规格，需要额外设计或逐一训练不同规格对应的策略。

Method: Logic-VLA包含三个核心组件：（1）基于语法图的STL编码器，在预训练阶段捕捉时序逻辑语义；（2）两阶段策略适配：先进行STL条件监督微调（在满足需求的演示数据上），再进行轨迹级偏好优化（在满足/违反需求的对齐rollout对上，使用流匹配代理的Identity Preference Optimization）；（3）推理时以STL规格为条件输入策略网络，实现单策略适应多种形式化需求。

Result: 在闭环四旋翼导航仿真（随机逼真环境）中，Logic-VLA相比不感知STL的基座策略，将STL满足率提升了24.8至40.7个百分点，同时自然语言任务成功率最多仅下降1.8个百分点，并展示了在训练中未见STL公式上的泛化能力。

Conclusion: Logic-VLA证明了单个VLA模型可以通过条件化形式化需求来调整行为，无需为每种规格训练独立策略，从而在保持自然语言任务能力的同时显著提升安全关键与时序约束的满足率。该方法在形式需求满足与名义任务性能之间取得了良好的权衡。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models can follow natural-language (NL) task instructions, but such instructions may not precisely specify safety-critical or spatiotemporal requirements on the resulting behavior. We introduce Logic-VLA, a formal-requirement-aware VLA that conditions on Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specifications supplied at inference time. Logic-VLA uses a syntax-graph-based STL encoder pre-trained to capture temporal logic semantics. Policy adaptation proceeds in two stages: STL-conditioned supervised fine-tuning on satisfying demonstrations is followed by trajectory-level preference optimization over matched satisfying-violating rollout pairs using a flow-matching surrogate for Identity Preference Optimization. This formulation improves formal requirement satisfaction while preserving the nominal NL task. We evaluate Logic-VLA in closed-loop quadcopter navigation simulation across randomized photorealistic environments and test generalization to STL formulas unseen during training. Across the evaluation benchmarks, Logic-VLA improves STL satisfaction rate over an STL-blind base policy by 24.8 to 40.7 percentage points (pp) while reducing nominal NL task success by at most 1.8 pp, showing that a single VLA can adapt its behavior to varying formal requirements without requiring a separate policy for each specification.

</details>


### [245] [Nonlinear Model Predictive Control for Trajectory Tracking of Differentially Flat Fixed-Wing Aerial Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20655)
*Nishanth Bobbili,Pratyaksh Rao,Luca Morando,Luca Masci,Giuseppe Loianno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，将基于微分平坦性的轨迹生成与非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）相结合，并引入风感知采样策略，用于固定翼无人机的规划与控制，提升在强风条件下的轨迹跟踪精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定翼无人机由于非线性动力学、气动极限和环境扰动，其规划与控制具有挑战性。微分平坦性虽可高效生成可行轨迹，但多局限于无模型控制器，缺乏预测能力且需要调参。因此需要一种既能高效规划又具备预测性和约束感知能力的统一框架。

Method: 提出统一框架整合两个关键技术：(1) 基于微分平坦性的轨迹生成，实现计算高效的运动规划；(2) 非线性模型预测控制（NMPC），提供预测性和约束感知的控制能力。此外，引入风感知采样策略嵌入NMPC框架中，生成动态可行且主动考虑风扰动的参考轨迹，同时严格满足气动和输入约束。

Result: 通过大量仿真和真实飞行实验验证，该框架在复杂轨迹上展现出改进的跟踪精度和鲁棒性，尤其是在强风条件下使用所提出的风感知采样策略时效果显著。

Conclusion: 该统一框架成功将微分平坦性规划的效率与NMPC的预测控制优势结合起来，并通过风感知采样进一步增强了系统在扰动环境下的鲁棒性，为固定翼无人机的自主飞行提供了一个有效的规划-控制一体化解决方案。

Abstract: Planning and control of fixed-wing Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) are challenging due to nonlinear dynamics, aerodynamic limits, and environmental disturbances. Differential flatness offers a principled way to generate fast, feasible trajectories, but its use has largely been confined to model-free controllers, which lack predictive capabilities and demand tuning. In this paper, we propose a unified framework that integrates differential flatness-based trajectory generation with Nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC), combining computationally efficient planning with predictive, constraint-aware control. To further improve robustness, we introduce a wind-aware sampling strategy embedded within the NMPC framework, enabling the generation of dynamically feasible reference trajectories that proactively account for wind disturbances while strictly enforcing aerodynamic and control input constraints. We validate the proposed framework through extensive simulations and real-world flight experiments, demonstrating improved tracking accuracy and robustness for complex trajectories, particularly when using the proposed wind-aware sampling strategy under strong wind conditions.

</details>


### [246] [Rethinking Demonstration Unlearning in Imitation Learning for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20784)
*Jiazhuo Li,Yu Zhang,Yiming Fei,Kangkang Dong,Xiaojun Zhu,Houde Liu,Jinze Tao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对机器人模仿学习中的"演示数据遗忘"问题，提出了一种基于重训练校准的审计方法，从行为（behavior）和证据（evidence）两个维度评估策略编辑是否真正实现了数据遗忘，并通过共形检验将两个维度结合为联合重训练一致性假设。实验在三个真实机器人策略类和两个仿真套件上验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人模仿学习中，用户可能要求删除某些人类演示数据。重新训练是自然基准但成本随策略和数据规模增长，因此需要更廉价的策略编辑操作符。然而现有的机器学习遗忘度量（如遗忘损失或单一成员攻击）无法有效评估闭环策略编辑究竟移除了什么，需要一个更可靠的审计框架。

Method: 提出双轴度的重训练校准审计：(1) 行为轴：测量编辑策略与剔除演示数据的重训练策略在匹配状态下的动作散度，并以独立重训练之间的散度作为地板进行校准；(2) 证据轴：对每个演示进行成员攻击，参照重训练零分布报告排名和绝对成员损失水平，防止仅看排名导致接受抬高损失的操作。最后用共形检验（conformal test）将两个轴度结合为联合重训练一致性假设，对照足够多的独立重训练集合以在常规显著性水平下做出判定。

Result: 在五个预注册条件下的三个真实机器人策略类和两个仿真套件上，两个轴度在某个检查点上双向分离：编辑可能修复任务行为但证据不变，或减少证据但行为偏离重训练。在ACT（Action Chunking with Transformers）策略上，重定向编辑将盲评机器人成功率恢复到20次试验中的18次。

Conclusion: 所提出的重训练校准审计能从行为和证据两个互补维度有效评估演示数据遗忘，揭示现有单一度量无法捕捉的编辑效果，为机器人策略数据删除提供了更可靠且经验证实的评估手段。

Abstract: Imitation learning for robotics depends on human demonstrations, some of which people may later ask to remove. Retraining without them is the natural reference, but its cost grows with policy and dataset scale, motivating cheaper operators that edit a trained policy. Metrics inherited from machine unlearning, such as forgetting loss or a single membership attack, do not establish what an edit removed from a policy acting in closed loop. We therefore introduce a retrain-calibrated audit that reads demonstration unlearning along two axes: behavior, whether the edited policy acts like one retrained without the removed demonstrations, and evidence, whether an auditor can still detect it was trained on them. The behavior axis measures action divergence to that retrain at matched states, calibrated by a floor built from independent retrains, so a policy at the floor is as close to a retrain as retrains are to each other. The evidence axis applies a per-demonstration membership attack against a retrain null, reporting both its rank and its absolute member-loss level, since rank alone accepts operators that inflate member losses past the null. A conformal test then combines both axes into one hypothesis of joint retrain consistency, against a fleet of independent retrains large enough to reject at conventional significance. Across five preregistered conditions on three real-robot policy classes and two simulation suites, the axes dissociate in both directions on one checkpoint, as an edit may repair task behavior while leaving evidence unchanged, or reduce evidence while moving behavior away from retraining. On the ACT arm, a redirect edit restores blind-scored robot success to 18 of 20 trials.

</details>


### [247] [Natural Sit-to-Stand Motion Synthesis For Humanoids via Guided Assistance Curricula and Staged Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20823)
*Meet Pal Singh,Vyankatesh Ashtekar,Ashish Dutta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于强化学习的双足人形机器人从坐姿到站姿（STS）运动合成方法，无需演示数据或参考轨迹，通过耦合力/椅高课程、基于逆运动学的姿态采样以及受生物力学启发的奖励设计，使单个PPO策略在八种椅高下实现超过97%的平衡站立成功率，并能从深度坐姿中自然起身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人从坐姿起身（STS）是一个具有无限可行轨迹的欠约束平衡控制难题，现有方法依赖演示或参考轨迹，缺乏从零合成自然、平滑且具有鲁棒性STS动作的手段。

Method: 方法包含三个互补组件：(i) 耦合的垂直骨盆辅助力与椅高递进课程，辅助力随训练衰减，逐步解锁更高椅子，避免过早分布偏移导致泛化崩溃；(ii) 利用大规模逆运动学生成的初始/目标姿态随机采样覆盖八种以上椅高，增强运动鲁棒性；(iii) 从生物力学和最优控制研究中提炼多组奖励，通过角动量塑造实现seat-off，并通过质心压力吸引函数促进支撑区域转移，实现平滑低耗力驱动。

Result: 在确定性无辅助力评估器上，策略在八种椅高下取得超过97%的平衡站立成功率；运动在不同椅高间平滑泛化，且能从显著深陷的坐姿中起身，优于现有技术。

Conclusion: 通过课程学习、姿态多样性采样和生物力学启发式奖励的有机结合，单个强化学习策略即可从零学习通用且自然的人形STS运动，为无参考轨迹的类人动作合成提供了有效范式。

Abstract: A humanoid has infinitely many ways to stand up from sitting while maintaining balance, making sit-to-stand (STS) a challenging control problem. We synthesise natural humanoid STS motion from scratch using reinforcement learning, without demonstrations or reference trajectories. A single Proximal Policy Optimisation policy learns smooth, human-like rising driven by three complementary components. (i) A coupled force/chair-height curriculum is used. A vertical pelvis-assist force aids early trajectory exploration and decays over training. Taller chairs are unlocked with decaying assisting force. This ensures that the policy masters a viable STS trajectory at each chair height before being exposed to harder ones, avoiding the premature distribution shift that otherwise collapses generalisation. (ii) Motion robustness is achieved by randomly sampling from a large number of inverse kinematics-generated initial and target poses spanning over eight chair heights. (iii) A set of rewards is defined inspired from biomechanics and optimal control studies. They shape the robot's angular momentum for seat-off, and enable support-region transition via centre of pressure attraction function to ensure smooth low-effort actuation. On a deterministic force-free evaluator, the policy attains more than 97% balanced-standing success across eight chair heights. The policy generalises smooth motion across chair heights and enables the robot to rise from substantially deep-seated postures as compared to the state of the art.

</details>


### [248] [Demonstration-Guided Humanoid Stand-Up on an Emulated Deformable Surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20852)
*Aniruddh Kushwah,Vyankatesh Ashtekar,Ashish Dutta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种参考引导的强化学习框架，使29自由度Unitree G1人形机器人在可变形软地面上完成站起动作。该方法利用硬地面录制的演示数据，通过调整MuJoCo软接触模型参数（solref/solimp）模拟地形柔顺度，结合参考轨迹跟踪与显式恢复奖励，先在硬地面训练再迁移至软地面，成功实现从摔倒到站立的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在真实世界中常需在柔软地形（如草地、沙地、垫子）上作业，而此类地形因接触面凹陷导致支撑力延迟产生，使得基于硬地面演示的强化学习训练策略难以直接迁移。现有方法缺乏对地形柔顺性的显式建模以及针对软地面站起这一具有挑战性任务的系统解决方案。

Method: 1) 使用MuJoCo的solref和solimp参数建模地形柔软度；2) 设计包含残差关节位置控制的参考运动跟踪奖励，以及骨盆高度、躯干直立度和最终姿态的显式恢复奖励；3) 两阶段训练策略：先在硬地面训练策略，再降低地形刚度（更新solref）并扩大名义表面穿透区（更新solimp）进行后续训练，使策略适应软地面接触穿透带来的支撑力延迟。

Result: 在仿真中成功完成摔倒到站立的任务，达到目标骨盆高度和躯干直立度，最大接触穿透约40mm。在两个站起序列上同时验证了硬地面和软地面的最终恢复目标达成。消融研究表明仅靠参考轨迹跟踪无法完成站起，显式恢复奖励是关键。

Conclusion: 提出的参考引导加显式恢复奖励的强化学习框架能够有效应对软地面延迟支撑力带来的挑战，实现了人形机器人在可变形地形上的站起动作泛化，为柔顺地形下的人形机器人运动生成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a reference-guided reinforcement learning framework to generate stand-up motion for a 29-DOF Unitree G1 humanoid on deformable soft ground, using a human demonstration recorded on hard ground. The terrain compliance is modelled using solref and solimp parameters from MuJoCo's rigid body soft-contact model. The rewards consists of (i) reference motion tracking through residual joint-position control and (ii) explicit recovery objectives such as pelvis height, torso uprightness, and the final posture. First, the policy is trained with the specified rewards considering hard ground. Next, the terrain stiffness is lowered by updating solref and the nominal surface penetration zone is expanded using solimp. Subsequent training enables the policy to adapt to the delayed support force generation due to significant surface penetration during contact-intensive phases while preserving the original demonstration pattern. The learned policy successfully completes the fallen-to-standing task in simulation, reaching the targeted pelvis height and uprightness, with a maximum contact penetration of approximately 40 mm during the process. The proposed method is demonstrated on two stand-up sequences and successfully achieves the final recovery objective on both hard and soft ground. Ablation studies show that reference tracking alone is insufficient for successful stand-up, and that explicit recovery rewards are essential.

</details>


### [249] [IMU-Free Body-Frame State Estimation with Sparse Scene Flow for Quadcopters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20891)
*Daniel Grønhaug,Sofie Markeset,Mathias Kolberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向X构型四旋翼的纯视觉状态估计系统，仅依赖立体相机图像和电机推力指令，无需任何惯性传感器。系统在机体坐标系中运行，通过连续-离散扩展卡尔曼滤波器在复合流形上估计位姿、速度、角速度、重力及扰动量，并输出稀疏点云与稀疏场景流，可作为下游机体坐标系世界模型的测量源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统四旋翼状态估计高度依赖GPS和IMU，但在GPS拒止环境或低成本平台中，惯性传感器不可靠或不可用。因此需要开发一种不依赖GPS、IMU或任何世界坐标系基础设施的纯视觉状态估计方案，以实现在受限环境中的自主飞行。

Method: 该系统：(1) 在复合流形状态⟨SE(3),ℝ³,...⟩上运行连续-离散EKF，以静止场景点为隐式惯性参考，估计机体坐标系下的位姿、速度、角速度、重力和扰动；(2) 使用FAST/Shi-Tomasi检测特征点，SSD/Lucas-Kanade进行时间跟踪，NCC进行跨相机匹配，由滤波器推导的位姿和点不确定性预测搜索区域；(3) 通过卡方门控归一化创新只接纳静止点；(4) 利用四视角（两个时间戳的双目对）全光束法平差，联合估计稀疏3D点云的位置和速度，以滤波器推导的相对位姿为先验；(5) 通过外部焦点引导空间自适应的点云密度分配。

Result: 系统能够输出机体坐标系状态估计、校准后的位姿变化和稀疏场景流，生成带有逐点位置、速度和联合协方差的稀疏3D点云。点云密度可在感兴趣区域实现密集覆盖而在其他区域保持稀疏，适用于下游以当前机体坐标系为锚点的世界模型。

Conclusion: 该纯视觉状态估计系统为无GPS、无IMU环境下的四旋翼飞行提供了一种完整解决方案，其机体坐标系架构天然支持未来GPS/IMU等外部传感器信息的融合扩展，具有较好的实用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: We present a vision-only state estimation system for X-configuration quadcopters equipped with a canonical stereo camera pair and no inertial sensors. The system operates entirely in the body frame, requiring only synchronised stereo images and motor thrust commands. A continuous-discrete extended Kalman filter on a composite manifold state $\langle SE(3), \mathbb{R}^3, \ldots \rangle$ maintains estimates of body-frame pose, velocity, angular velocity, gravity, and disturbances, using stationary scene points as implicit inertial references. Feature points are detected (FAST, Shi-Tomasi), tracked temporally (SSD, Lucas-Kanade) and matched across cameras (NCC), with search regions predicted from filter-derived pose and point uncertainty. Chi-squared gating on the normalised innovation admits only stationary points to the filter. The system also produces a sparse 3D point cloud carrying per-point position, velocity and joint covariance. These come from a 4-view (two stereo pairs at two timestamps) full bundle adjustment that jointly estimates position and velocity from stereo disparity and temporal parallax, with the filter-derived relative pose as a prior. Feature points in the EKF do not enter the solver; their information is reflected through the pose prior. Point cloud density is spatially adaptive: an external focus point directs allocation, producing dense coverage in the region of attention and sparse coverage elsewhere. The output is a body-frame state estimate, a calibrated pose change, and a sparse scene flow. It is intended as a measurement source for a downstream world model anchored in the current body frame, without dependence on GPS, IMU, or any world-frame infrastructure, though the architecture accommodates their future integration.

</details>


### [250] [Scalable Distributed Simulation-Based Testing for Automated Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20904)
*Christian Geller,Benedikt Haas,Lutz Eckstein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个端到端的DevOps驱动框架，用于在轻量级Kubernetes集群上自动化CARLA场景测试的构建、部署和分布式执行，在200个场景的多节点K3s集群评测中实现了超过8倍的端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于虚拟场景的测试是验证自动驾驶系统和智能交通系统的关键手段，但大规模场景测试套件（可能包含数千个场景）的执行劳动密集且难以扩展，亟需自动化、可扩展的测试基础设施。

Method: 构建了一个DevOps驱动的端到端框架：将ROS 2应用打包为标准Kubernetes Helm charts（从仓库规范自动生成），通过动态Helmfile清单以声明式方式组合整个仿真环境，并利用Argo Workflows实现分布式测试工作流，包括环境供给、OpenSCENARIO测试用例的聚合与批处理、跨集群节点并行执行场景以及日志和资源指标收集。

Result: 在运行200个场景的多节点K3s集群上，最佳配置相比顺序基线将端到端工作流时间加速了超过8倍，并量化了并行度、编排开销与集群稳定性之间的权衡。该框架还在真实ADS测试应用中得到了验证，展示了与场景源和下游评估模块的连接。

Conclusion: 该框架为可扩展的仿真测试提供了坚实基础，不仅能显著提升执行效率，还能生成可追溯的证据以支持安全论证。

Abstract: Virtual scenario-based testing is a key enabler for validating automated driving systems (ADS) and intelligent transport systems (ITS). However, executing large-scale test suites involving possibly thousands of scenarios remains labor-intensive and difficult to scale. This paper presents an end-to-end, DevOps-driven framework that automates build, deployment, and distributed execution of CARLA-based scenario tests of an ADS on a lightweight Kubernetes cluster. ROS 2 applications are packaged as standardized Kubernetes Helm charts generated from repository specifications, while entire simulation environments are composed declaratively via dynamic Helmfile manifests. The paper describes how a distributed testing workflow can be implemented in Argo Workflows to provision environments, aggregate and batch OpenSCENARIO test cases from configurable sources, execute scenarios in parallel across cluster nodes, and collect logs and resource metrics. In an evaluation on a multi-node K3s cluster running 200 scenarios, the best configuration speeds up end-to-end workflow time by more than a factor of eight compared to a sequential baseline. The results demonstrate significant gains in end-to-end execution time and quantify trade-offs between parallelism, orchestration overhead, and cluster stability. The framework is further demonstrated in a real-world ADS test application with connections to scenario sources and downstream evaluation modules. This demonstrates that the approach provides a strong foundation not only for scalable simulation testing, but also for generating traceable evidence that can support safety arguments.

</details>


### [251] [Fast Coordinated Bimanual Motion Planning With Hard Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20946)
*Borna Paro,Luka Petrović,Ivan Marković*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种快速的双手操作运动规划流水线，采用领导者-跟随者参数化方法，将硬刚体变换约束沿整条路径连续强制满足，在仿真中比先前方法快19.4倍，并通过真实双臂Kinova Gen3实验验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双手操作虽能处理复杂任务，但高自由度带来的复杂度显著增加。处理刚性物体时，两个末端执行器之间的相对变换必须在整个运动中保持固定，这表现为非线性等式约束，将可行配置空间限制为零测度量流形，给传统运动规划器带来巨大挑战。

Method: 提出领导者-跟随者参数化方法：领导者的配置作为自由变量，跟随者的配置通过逆运动学求解以满足硬变换约束。该约束沿整条路径连续地强制执行，而非仅在离散点上满足。

Result: 在多种仿真环境、约束条件和双臂平台上进行了广泛评估，规划速度比先前方法快19.4倍，同时保证连续约束满足。在双臂Kinova Gen3平台上进行了真实世界实验（包括托盘搬运和细长物体操作），验证了规划轨迹可直接迁移到物理硬件。

Conclusion: 所提出的流水线在保持严格约束满足的同时大幅提升了规划效率，且规划结果可直接部署于物理硬件，为刚性物体双手操作提供了高效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Bimanual manipulation enables complex tasks but introduces added complexity from the high number of degrees of freedom involved. When handling rigid objects, the relative transformation between the two end effectors must remain fixed throughout the motion, manifesting as a nonlinear equality constraint that confines the feasible configuration space to a measure-zero manifold and challenges conventional motion planners. We propose a fast bimanual motion planning pipeline that enforces this hard transformation constraint continuously along the entire path, using a leader-follower parameterization: the leader's configuration is treated as a free variable, while the follower's is determined via inverse kinematics to satisfy the constraint. We extensively evaluate the method in simulation across diverse environments, constraints and bimanual platforms, achieving 19.4x faster planning than prior work while guaranteeing continuous constraint satisfaction. Real-world experiments on a bimanual Kinova Gen3 setup, involving tray transport and elongated-object manipulation, validate direct transfer of planned trajectories to physical hardware.

</details>


### [252] [Neural-Primitive: An Efficient End-to-end Local Planner with Primitive-based Imitation Learning for Autonomous Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20948)
*Zhitao Liu,Guangtong Xu,Zihan Wang,Jialiang Hou,Chao Xu,Fei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于模仿学习的端到端局部规划器，通过轻量级离线原语数据集收集框架生成安全高质量轨迹原语，并用紧凑神经网络将传感器输入直接映射为多项式系数，无需后端求解即可在未知密集环境中实现实时、平滑、无碰撞且动态可行的自主飞行，计算时间低于1ms（桌面）和平均3.68ms（机载），内存占用小于1.5MiB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未知密集环境中的自主飞行面临机载轨迹生成在计算效率、轨迹质量和内存占用三者之间难以兼顾的困境，现有方法无法同时满足实时性与高质量轨迹生成的需求。

Method: 设计轻量级离线原语（offline-primitive）数据集收集框架，在非凸环境中生成安全、高质量的轨迹原语；训练一个紧凑神经网络，将传感器输入直接映射为多项式系数（隐含高阶动力学信息），实现端到端、无需后端求解的局部规划。

Result: 在标准桌面上计算时间低于1ms，机载飞行平均3.68ms，内存占用小于1.5MiB；仿真基准测试在规划延迟和目标到达进展质量上均优于对比方法；真实世界零样本部署验证了良好的仿真到现实迁移能力。

Conclusion: 该方法通过模仿学习与离线轨迹原语相结合，有效化解了计算-质量-内存三维度权衡难题，实现了高效、轻量且具备鲁棒迁移能力的端到端飞行规划方案。

Abstract: Autonomous flight in unknown cluttered environments is hindered by the computation-quality-memory trilemma of onboard trajectory generation. In this paper, we propose an efficient end-to-end local planner via imitation learning. A lightweight offline-primitive-based dataset collection framework is designed to produce safe and high-quality trajectory primitives in non-convex environments. A compact neural network directly maps sensory inputs to polynomial coefficients that inherently encode higher-order dynamical information. The learned policy generates smooth, empirically collision-free and dynamically feasible trajectories in real time without back-end solving. It achieves ultra-fast computation (below 1ms on a standard desktop and average 3.68ms during onboard flight), while maintaining low onboard memory requirements (less than 1.5MiB). Extensive simulation benchmarks demonstrate superiority in both planning latency and target-reaching progress quality. Zero-shot deployment in real-world experiments further validates the robust sim-to-real transfer capability of the proposed method.

</details>


### [253] [Hybrid Roller-Jamming Gripper for Object Acquisition and Retention Under Pose Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20962)
*Yijie Ren,Guillaume Gourmelen,Hiroyasu Iwata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合滚轮-颗粒阻塞（hybrid roller-jamming）夹具，将主动物体吸入与抓取后保持功能集成于单一夹具中。通过内旋滚轮增加接触并将物体拉向夹具中心，随后利用真空诱导的颗粒阻塞使滚轮硬化并稳定抓取。在840次抓取试验中成功812次（平面偏移215/216，方向偏移597/624），消融实验表明滚轮与颗粒阻塞各自贡献不同，为改善非理想首次接触后的获取与保持提供了机制级证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭操作中，姿态不确定性常导致偏心或部分初始接触，使可靠物体获取变得困难。滚轮式夹具能主动将物体拉入但抓取后稳定性有限，而颗粒阻塞夹具需要足够的接触才能实现强力保持。因此需要一种能兼顾主动吸入与抓取后保持的解决方案。

Method: 设计了一种混合滚轮-颗粒阻塞夹具：内旋滚轮转动以增加接触并将物体拉向夹具中心，随后通过真空诱导颗粒阻塞使滚轮硬化，实现抓取稳定。同时提出简化的几何分析、台架级力能力表征，并在7-DoF机械臂上使用8个测试对象进行评估，应用受控的平面位置和方向偏移，进行840次主评估试验和162次消融试验。

Result: 主评估中夹具在840次试验中成功812次：平面偏移215/216，方向偏移597/624。消融评估中，滚轮单独模式和颗粒阻塞单独模式分别取得54/81和24/81的成功率，表明两种机制各具不同贡献。

Conclusion: 初步机制级证据表明，混合滚轮-颗粒阻塞策略是改善不完全首次接触后物体获取与保持的一种有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: In household manipulation, pose uncertainty often results in off-centre or partial initial contact, making reliable object acquisition difficult. Roller-based grippers can actively draw objects inward but often provide limited post-capture stability, whereas granular-jamming grippers require sufficient contact before jamming to achieve strong retention. This paper presents a hybrid roller-jamming gripper that integrates active object intake and post-capture retention within a single gripper. The proposed gripper uses inward roller rotation to increase contact and draw the object toward the gripper centre, followed by vacuum-induced granular jamming to stiffen the rollers and stabilise the grasp. The paper also presents a simplified geometric analysis of the gripper and a bench-level characterisation of the prototype's force capability. The gripper prototype was mounted on a 7-DoF robotic arm and evaluated using eight test objects. Furthermore, controlled planar position and orientation offsets were applied, with each condition repeated three times. The main evaluation comprised 840 grasp trials, including 216 planar-offset trials and 624 orientation-offset trials. Overall, the gripper succeeded in 812/840 trials: 215/216 planar-offset trials and 597/624 orientation-offset trials. The ablation evaluation comprised 162 trials on three objects. The roller-only and jamming-only conditions achieved 54/81 and 24/81 successes, respectively, showing their different contributions. These results provide initial mechanism-level evidence that hybrid roller-jamming is a promising strategy for improving acquisition and retention after imperfect first contact.

</details>


### [254] [PhysCaP: Grounding Code-as-Policy Agent with Physics-Informed Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21031)
*Chen-Yu Lin,Jing-Wen Chen,Hsueh-En Chang,Hung-An Chen,Sheng-Hsun Chang,Chi-Pin Huang,Fu-En Yang,Min-Hung Chen,Yi-Ting Chen,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang,Shao-Hua Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysCaP是一个物理信息驱动的代码即策略智能体，通过引入物理信息探索层来增强机器人操作中的主动感知。它利用无需训练的物理属性提取模块从机器人本体感觉中估计物体质量与刚度，并采用双智能体架构（Planner决定何时探索与停止，Prioritizer过滤不合理交互并按优先级排序）来平衡探索成本与信息效率。实验表明该方法在真实桌面操作任务和LIBERO仿真任务中性能可比但交互次数更少、执行时间更短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作策略虽然擅长模仿示范，但依赖被动观察，无法推断对操作至关重要的潜在物理属性（如质量、刚度）。这导致机器人在面对隐藏物理属性的任务时表现不佳，需要引入主动感知与信息寻求机制。

Method: PhysCaP在代码即策略框架上增加了物理信息探索层：1）提出无需训练、无需额外传感器的物理属性提取模块，通过机器人本体感觉估计物体质量与刚度；2）采用双智能体设计，Planner决定何时进行探索或停止，Prioritizer过滤不合理的交互候选并用启发式优先级分数排序，实现高效有针对性的探索。

Result: 在真实桌面操作任务（搜索隐藏物体、检测空罐、寻找成熟牛油果）和LIBERO仿真任务上的实验表明，现有的被动式和朴素交互基线在物理属性隐藏时会失败或过度探索，而PhysCaP在更少的交互次数和更短的执行时间内达到可比性能。消融实验验证了物理属性提取模块的有效性。

Conclusion: PhysCaP证明了将物理信息融入代码即策略框架能够显著提升机器人操作的主动感知能力，通过受控的交互相较于被动策略更高效地推断隐藏物理属性，为操作中主动感知提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: We present PhysCaP, a Physics-Informed Code-as-Policy agent for active perception in robotic manipulation. While vision-language-action policies excel at imitating demonstrations, they rely on passive observation and fail to infer latent physical properties critical for manipulation. PhysCaP augments code-as-policy frameworks with a physics-informed exploration layer that enables explicit information-seeking through interaction. It introduces training-free physical property extraction modules that estimate object mass and stiffness from robot proprioception without additional sensors. To balance exploration costs and the efficiency of information obtained, PhysCaP employs a dual-agent design: a Planner that decides when to explore and when to stop, and a Prioritizer that filters implausible interactions and ranks the remainder using a heuristic priority score, enabling efficient, targeted exploration. We evaluate PhysCaP on real-world tabletop manipulation tasks (searching for hidden objects, detecting empty cans, and finding ripe avocados) and a simulated task in LIBERO. The results show that existing passive and naive interactive baselines either fail when physical properties are hidden or over-explore, whereas PhysCaP achieves comparable performance with fewer interactions and reduced execution time. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of the proposed physical property extraction modules. Project page: https://physcap.github.io

</details>


### [255] [Roadside-Cooperative Autonomous Driving: From Data Platform to Vision-Language End-to-End Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21032)
*Yitao Xu,Tong Wu,Yiyan Wu,Guoji Xu,Yanbo Jiang,Jiahao Wang,Zehong Ke,Junkai Jiang,Fang Zhang,Jianqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了V2XBench仿真平台和Chat-V2XBench数据集，并在此基础上构建了AURORA端到端协同驾驶框架，通过跨视角查询对齐与融合模块（CQAF）和LoRA适配的视觉语言模型（VLM），实现了在严重遮挡场景下超越现有方法的协同自动驾驶性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有V2X基准缺乏闭环评估和语言引导监督，阻碍了视觉语言模型在端到端协同驾驶中的发展。

Method: 1) 构建V2XBench仿真平台，支持同步自车-路侧感知和闭环评估；2) 构建Chat-V2XBench渐进式结构化VQA数据集，用于协同推理；3) 提出AURORA框架，包含双视角感知架构、查询级跨视角查询对齐与融合模块（CQAF）、LoRA适配的VLM实现语义推理与生成式轨迹规划的桥接。

Result: 在V2XBench上闭环评估中，AURORA在严重遮挡场景下达到最先进性能：路线完成率98.21%，驾驶评分76.02，且需要的路侧通信带宽较低。

Conclusion: 该工作开创了可扩展的V2X-VLM范式，为下一代协同自动驾驶铺平道路。

Abstract: Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) cooperation enables beyond-line-of-sight perception, mitigating occlusions in single-vehicle sensing. However, existing V2X benchmarks provide limited support for closed-loop evaluation and language-grounded supervision, hindering the development of vision-language models (VLMs) for end-to-end cooperative driving. To address these limitations, we introduce V2XBench, a simulation platform featuring synchronized ego--roadside sensing and closed-loop evaluation, together with Chat-V2XBench, a progressively structured VQA dataset for cooperative reasoning. Building upon this benchmark infrastructure, we propose AURORA, an end-to-end cooperative driving framework. Equipped with a dual-view perception architecture, AURORA mitigates spatial and semantic discrepancies across ego and roadside viewpoints through a query-level Cross-View Query Alignment and Fusion (CQAF) module. Leveraging the resulting unified tokens, a LoRA-adapted VLM bridges semantic reasoning and generative trajectory planning. Extensive closed-loop evaluations on V2XBench demonstrate that AURORA achieves state-of-the-art performance in heavily occluded scenarios, with a Route Completion rate of 98.21% and a Driving Score of 76.02, while requiring low roadside communication bandwidth. Ultimately, this work pioneers an extensible V2X--VLM paradigm, paving the way for next-generation cooperative autonomous driving.

</details>


### [256] [TaPeR: Probabilistic Recovery of Sparse Task Precedence Graphs from a Handful of Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21035)
*Adrian Röfer,Karla Stepanova,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从人类演示中提取长时程操作任务依赖结构的方法，仅利用简单运动学图和物体相对位姿分布，无需显式符号谓词标注。该方法首先估计成对任务步骤依赖概率来初始化前序图边权重，再通过过滤管线生成最终任务依赖图，能够从更少的演示中恢复更准确的任务结构，并支持生成多种有效的机器人执行顺序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程操作任务通常是部分有序的（例如组装电子设备时电池和电路板可任意顺序安装，但两者都必须在封闭外壳之前完成）。恢复这种任务依赖关系能让机器人灵活重排子任务而不破坏任务有效性。现有方法通常依赖人类演示中的时序和符号监督，但符号谓词需要显式接地（grounding），在现实场景中难以获得。

Method: 该方法仅使用简单运动学图和物体相对位姿分布来提取任务依赖结构。具体步骤包括：（1）从演示中估计成对任务步骤间的依赖概率；（2）利用这些概率初始化前序图（precedence graph）的边权重；（3）引入过滤管线，将概率估计图转换为最终的任务依赖图。

Result: 在现有基准测试和一个包含更长任务和更复杂依赖关系的新数据集上评估，该方法相比基线方法能从更少的演示中恢复更准确的任务结构。此外，推断出的图可用于为同一任务生成多种有效的机器人执行顺序。

Conclusion: 本文证明了仅从运动学图和物体相对位姿分布中获取任务依赖结构是可行的，避免了符号谓词接地的困难，为机器人灵活重排子任务提供了有效且实用的途径。

Abstract: Long-horizon manipulation tasks are often only partially ordered. For example, when assembling an electronic device, the battery and circuit board may be installed in either order, but both must be in place before the enclosure is closed. Recovering such dependencies enables robots to flexibly reorder subtasks while preserving task validity. Existing approaches typically infer task structure from human demonstrations using both temporal and symbolic supervision. However, symbolic predicates require explicit grounding, which is difficult to obtain in realistic settings. In this work, we present an approach for extracting task dependency structures from demonstrations using only simple kinematic graphs and distributions over relative object poses. From these representations, our method estimates pairwise task-step-dependency probabilities and uses them to initialize the edge weights of a precedence graph. We then introduce a filtering pipeline that converts this graph of probability estimates into the final task dependency graph. We evaluate our approach on an existing benchmark and on a new dataset comprising longer tasks with more complex dependencies. We find that our method recovers more accurate task structures from fewer demonstrations than the baselines. Finally, we demonstrate that the inferred graphs can be used to generate multiple valid robotic execution orders for the same task.

</details>


### [257] [FF-MPCC: High-speed Agile Formation Flight with Model Predictive Contouring Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21056)
*Aditya Dandwate,Vit Kratky,Parakh M. Gupta,Martin Saska,Robert Penicka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种新颖的去中心化编队飞行方法，将编队保持集成到MPCC框架中，使无人机沿复杂路径自适应行进同时满足个体动力学约束，在高达21 m/s的高速飞行中比时间参数化轨迹跟踪改进了65%的编队保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机沿复杂轨迹高速飞行时保持规定编队形状仍然是一个挑战性问题，尤其是在需要以平台极限飞行的苛刻轨迹下，传统时间参数化轨迹跟踪方法难以在高速条件下同时保证编队完整性和个体动力学约束。

Method: 提出一种去中心化的编队飞行方法：将编队保持集成到MPCC（Model Predictive Contouring Control）框架中；引入动态编队几何的重新参数化和同步方法；采用去中心化方法确定各无人机期望位置，使编队沿路径协调高速跟随时自适应调整行进速度。

Result: 通过大量仿真和真实世界实验验证（涵盖不同路径复杂度和飞行中编队形状变化场景），在高达21 m/s的高速飞行中，相比时间参数化轨迹跟踪方法，编队保持精度提升了65%，同时到达目标的时间相当。

Conclusion: 提出的方法能够在高速条件下有效协调编队沿路径飞行，在不牺牲编队完整性的前提下实现敏捷编队飞行，验证了其在复杂路径和动态编队形状变化场景中的鲁棒性和实用性。

Abstract: Flying in a prescribed formation in an agile manner remains a challenging problem in the field of UAVs, particularly when following highly-demanding trajectories that require flight at platform limits. We address this problem by proposing a novel decentralized approach to formation flight along a given path that integrates formation maintenance into the MPCC framework, allowing UAVs to adapt their progression along complex paths while respecting individual dynamic constraints and maintaining the desired formation. To this end, we introduce a novel reparametrization and synchronization method for dynamic formation geometries together with a decentralized approach to determine the desired positions for the individual UAVs. The proposed approach allows the formation to coordinate high-speed path following without compromising formation integrity. The proposed approach is validated through extensive simulation and real-world experiments involving scenarios with varying complexity of paths and changes of required formation shape on the fly. In comparison to time-parameterized trajectory tracking, we demonstrate improved formation maintenance by 65% in high-speed flight with velocities up to 21 m/s, while achieving comparable times required to reach the goal.

</details>


### [258] [Teaching is a Process: The TOSS Framework for Modeling Human Teaching Decisions in Human-Interactive Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21083)
*Bernhard Hilpert,Kim Baraka,Joost Broekens*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过一个N=34的探索性自下而上研究，分析了204条人类在观察机器人强化学习场景时的直觉教学反应，揭示了教学决策是由触发因素、目标、信号和策略组成的互联网络，并提出了TOSS框架来概念化人机教学作为机器人行为与人类教学行动之间的程序化循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成功的人机教学依赖于机器人处理需求与人类教学意图之间的一致性，但当前对人们在教机器人时直觉应用的内在逻辑理解不足，需要揭示这一潜在逻辑以改进人机协作教学系统。

Method: 采用探索性、自下而上的研究方法，招募34名参与者在两种不同的机器人强化学习场景中观察，收集204条跨早期、中期、晚期学习阶段的教学反应并进行分析，识别出教学决策中的触发因素、目标、信号和策略及其之间的互联关系。

Result: 发现教学决策是一个由触发因素（情境催化剂）、目标（主观教学目标）、信号（沟通行为）和策略（高层治理）组成的微妙互联网络；教师会自发扮演教练、工程师或设计师等多样化角色并优先考虑不同目标。基于此提出TOSS框架，将人机教学概念化为机器人行为与人类教学行动之间的程序化循环。

Conclusion: TOSS框架为理解教学决策、模拟现实教学预言者以及设计以人为中心的教学设置和新颖的机器人学习算法提供了公开数据集和理论基础，超越了当前机器人学习设置的局限。

Abstract: Successful Human-Robot Teaching assumes alignment between robot processing needs and human teaching intent. To better understand this alignment, this work seeks to uncover the underlying logic that humans intuitively apply when teaching. Through an exploratory, bottom-up study with N=34, participants observing two distinct robot Reinforcement Learning (RL) scenarios, we analyze 204 intuitive teaching responses across early, middle, and late learning phases. Results reveal that teaching decisions consist of a nuanced, interconnected network of Triggers (situational catalysts), Objectives (subjective teaching targets), Signals (communicative acts), and Strategies (high-level governance) in which teachers spontaneously adopt diverse roles, acting as coaches, engineers, or designers and prioritize different objectives. Based on these results, we introduce the TOSS Framework, which conceptualizes Human-Robot teaching as a procedural loop between robot behavior and human teaching actions, in which human teaching decisions are modeled as Trigger-Signal responses modulated by teaching Objectives and Strategies. It provides future research with an openly accessible dataset and a theoretical foundation for a) understanding teaching decisions and b) simulating realistic oracles as well as c) designing human-centered teaching settings and novel robot learning algorithms that go beyond the constraints of current robot learning settings.

</details>


### [259] [SRL-MPC: Shape-Aware Reinforcement Learned Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21175)
*Ruihua Han,Rui Gao,Zhe Liu,Xinyi Wang,Chang Chen,Shuai Wang,Qi Hao,Jia Pan,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SRL-MPC框架，结合高阶层控制障碍函数（HOCBF）与强化学习（RL），实现异构形状机器人群在密集人群中的安全、高效、自适应导航，无需几何简化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统形状感知导航方法通常假设同质机器人、稀疏工作空间、简化几何、离线计算或手工调参，限制了其在密集人群场景中的部署。异构形状机器人在多变人群中的安全高效导航仍具挑战。

Method: 基于支撑函数变换从几何分离特征（GSFs）构造高阶层控制障碍函数（HOCBF）约束以编码形状感知安全；利用强化学习（RL）训练神经网络策略读取GSFs并实时输出MPC参数更新，使MPC求解器自适应邻近人群几何形状。

Result: 在随机人群场景与任意形状机器人编队实验中，SRL-MPC在安全性和自适应性上显著优于代表性基线方法，验证了其有效性、可扩展性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SRL-MPC通过融合MPC的安全结构与泛化能力以及RL的自适应与智能性，在无需几何简化的前提下实现了异构形状机器人在密集人群中的安全高效导航，兼顾安全性与适应性。

Abstract: Safe and efficient shape-aware navigation in heterogeneous crowds and robot fleets remains challenging. Traditional approaches often assume homogeneous robots, sparse workspaces, simplified geometry, offline computation, or handcrafted parameters to make the problem tractable, which limits their deployment in dense crowd scenarios. Toward this end, we propose Shape-Aware Reinforcement Learned Model Predictive Control (SRL-MPC), a method for safe, efficient, and adaptive navigation in crowds with heterogeneous shapes without geometry simplification. To encode shape-aware safety, we formulate high-order control barrier function (HOCBF) constraints from geometric separation features (GSFs) based on support function transformation. A reinforcement learning (RL) framework then learns a neural policy that reads GSFs and outputs real-time MPC parameter updates, enabling the MPC solver to adapt to neighboring crowd geometries. The key advantage of SRL-MPC is that it preserves the safety structure and generalizability of MPC while integrating the adaptability and intelligence of RL. Experiments in randomized crowd scenarios with arbitrary shaped robot fleets demonstrate the effectiveness, scalability, and robustness of SRL-MPC. The results show that SRL-MPC substantially outperforms representative baselines in safety and adaptability. Project website: https://hanruihua.github.io/srl_mpc_project/

</details>


### [260] [Beyond Imitation: Self-Improving Robot Policies via Off-Policy Q-Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21204)
*Varun Giridhar,Anant Khandelwal,Jeremy A. Collins,Ignat Georgiev,Animesh Garg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Q-Planning方法，通过小规模的离策略Q函数为大规模视觉运动行为克隆策略提供价值引导的动作选择与在线自我改进能力，无需训练辅助actor，在仿真与真实机器人任务上均显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆（BC）无法自我改进：策略失败时无法从失败中学习，而强化学习微调难以扩展到数十亿参数规模的现代机器人策略模型。

Method: Q-Planning为大型BC策略配备一个小规模离策略Q函数。由于Q函数估计价值而非模仿动作，可同时利用成功演示和失败的部署轨迹进行训练。推理时通过单步Q加权平均对BC采样进行价值引导选择；在线自改进仅微调Q函数，BC权重保持不变。

Result: LIBERO-10成功率从93%提升至99%，RoboTwin从83.8%提升至91.4%；真实双臂接触丰富任务中，stack-cups从40%提升至90%，insert-wallet从25%提升至80%（五轮迭代），而基于成功rollout的SFT仅分别停滞在55%和30%。在相同在线预算下，Q-Planning是唯一能稳定从失败中学习且无需训练辅助actor的方法。

Conclusion: Q-Planning利用BC与Q函数之间的"价值vs模仿"不对称性，实现了仅微调Q函数的轻量级在线自我改进范式，在保持大规模BC策略冻结的同时获得稳定性能提升，为机器人策略的自改进提供了一条可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Behaviour Cloning (BC) has driven remarkable progress in robot manipulation, yet it is fundamentally limited by its inability to self-improve: a policy that fails cannot learn from that failure without additional human demonstrations. Reinforcement Learning fine-tuning offers a path to self-improvement but has proven difficult to scale to the multi-billion-parameter models underpinning modern robot policies. We propose Q-Planning, which equips a large visuomotor BC policy with a small off-policy Q-function. Because a Q-function estimates value rather than imitates actions, it can be trained on the same successful demonstrations as the BC policy and later absorb both successful and failed deployment rollouts, an asymmetry BC does not have. We exploit this asymmetry to enable value-guided action selection at inference (a single-step Q-weighted average over BC draws) and online self-improvement that fine-tunes only the Q-function, leaving the BC weights untouched. On LIBERO and bimanual RoboTwin, ten iterations of self-improvement lift every benchmark score we tested (LIBERO-10 93% to 99%, RoboTwin 83.8% to 91.4%) and shorten successful episodes on the near-ceiling suites (LIBERO-Object, LIBERO-Goal). On two contact-rich bimanual real-robot tasks, the same loop (BC frozen, no human intervention) improves purely from its own deployment rollouts: stack-cups 40% to 90% and insert-wallet 25% to 80% in five iterations, whereas SFT on successful rollouts alone stalls at 55% and 30%. Under an identical online budget Q-Planning is the only method, among Best-of-N, filtered SFT, IBRL, DSRL, and DAWR, that improves stably from failures without training an auxiliary actor.

</details>


### [261] [The Coastline as a Structural Constraint: Harnessing Scene Geometry for Autonomous Surface Vessel Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21276)
*Derek R. Benham,Joshua G. Mangelson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出两种利用海岸线和水面几何结构进行GPS拒止环境下无人船自主定位的框架：一种基于LiDAR估计横滚、俯仰和升沉并配准海岸线与卫星地图；另一种基于单目被动影像进行语义分割检测海岸线和地平线，通过层次因子图融合局部子图实现定位。三种真实沿海数据集实验表明LiDAR方案优于基线，单目方案长期漂移有界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沿海环境蕴含丰富且尚未充分开发的几何结构，可为GPS拒止环境下的无人船提供全局参考定位线索。现有方法在无GPS条件下难以实现可靠的长航时海洋定位，亟需利用海岸天然几何特征构建不依赖外部导航信号的定位方案。

Method: 第一个框架：利用LiDAR对水面观测估计横滚（roll）、俯仰（pitch）和升沉（heave），通过对海岸线观测与卫星海岸线地图的配准恢复全局位置和航向。第二个框架：仅依赖被动影像，通过语义分割检测海岸线和地平线，利用海岸场景几何从单目图像推断海岸线距离，将观测累积为短期局部子图，与同一卫星海岸线地图配准，并在层次因子图中融合。

Result: 在三个真实世界沿海数据集上的评估显示：LiDAR管线在轨迹精度上持续优于标准基线；单目架构保持有界的长时漂移。此外，现代零样本基础模型能够可靠地在多种沿海环境中提取海岸线观测。

Conclusion: 沿海几何结构为GPS拒止海事定位提供了强大且可靠的全局参考信息来源。LiDAR与单目两种互补框架均验证了利用海岸线几何实现自主定位的可行性，证明了该方法在无GPS环境下的实用价值。

Abstract: Coastal environments contain rich, largely unexploited geometric structure capable of providing globally referenced localization cues. In this work, we present two complementary localization frameworks that exploit shoreline and water-surface geometry for GPS-denied autonomous surface vessel localization. The first framework leverages LiDAR observations of the water surface to estimate roll, pitch, and heave (vertical motion), while recovering global position and heading through direct registration of shoreline observations against a satellite-derived coastline map. The second framework relies solely on passive imagery to detect the shoreline and horizon through semantic segmentation. Using the proposed coastal scene geometry, shoreline distance is inferred from monocular imagery. Shoreline observations are accumulated into short-duration local submaps, registered against the same satellite-derived coastline map, and fused within a hierarchical factor graph. Evaluated across three real-world coastal datasets, the LiDAR pipeline consistently improves trajectory accuracy over standard baselines, while the monocular architecture maintains bounded long-term drift. In addition, we establish that modern zero-shot foundation models can reliably extract shoreline observations across diverse coastal environments. Together, these results demonstrate that coastal geometry provides a powerful and dependable source of globally referenced information for GPS-denied maritime localization.

</details>


### [262] [VT-MUSE: Multimodal Unified Sequential Visuotactile Representation Learning for Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21290)
*Congsheng Xu,Qiaochu Yang,Fangyuan Shi,Yifan Han,Baijun Chen,Yiming Wang,Haonan Zhao,Daolin Ma,Xiaokang Yang,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VT-MUSE，一种面向视觉-触觉操作的多模态统一序列表示学习框架，通过两阶段学习（跨模态时序对齐+条件变分潜变量建模）捕捉细粒度跨模态依赖与时间演化，在仿真和真实实验中显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常在融合前独立编码视觉和触觉观测，难以捕捉细粒度的跨模态依赖；且多数方法仅关注当前时间步的观测，忽略接触的时间演化信息。这两个限制阻碍了机器人操作中对多模态时序信息的高效利用。

Method: 两阶段表示学习框架。Stage I：通过跨模态时序对齐和掩码视图一致性联合适配模态特定编码器；Stage II：条件变分潜变量模型处理掩码视觉序列与完整触觉历史，辅助解码器重构最近被掩码的视觉观测并预测触觉深度变化，使潜表示保留全局视觉上下文与局部接触动力学。学习到的表示通过门控交叉注意力集成到轻量级Transformer策略中。

Result: 在仿真基准上，VT-MUSE在所有任务上比最强基线平均高出11个百分点；在真实世界实验中同样取得了显著改进。

Conclusion: 明确验证了融合跨模态时序对齐与条件变分建模的多模态统一序列表示学习能够有效提升视觉-触觉操作任务的性能，为机器人精细操作提供了一种可靠的表示学习范式。

Abstract: We propose VT-MUSE, a Multimodal Unified SEquential representation learning framework for visuotactilemanipulation. Existing approaches often encode visual and tactile observations independently before fusion, limiting their ability to capture fine-grained cross-modal dependencies. Moreover, most methods focus on observations at the current time step and overlook the temporal evolution of contact. VT-MUSE addresses both limitations through a two-stage representation learning framework. In Stage I, modality specific encoders are jointly adapted via cross-modal temporal alignment and masked-view consistency. In Stage II, a conditional variational latent model processes masked visual sequences together with full tactile histories. Auxiliary decoders reconstruct the masked recent visual observations and predict tactile depth changes, encouraging the latent representation to retain both global visual context and local contact dynamics. The learned representation is subsequently integrated into a lightweight Transformer policy through gated cross-attention. On the simulation benchmark, VT-MUSE outperforms the strongest baseline evaluated on all tasks by 11 percentage points and also achieves substantial improvements in real-world experiments.

</details>


### [263] [NeSAM: Neuro-Symbolic Kinodynamics with Soil Adaptation for Off-Road Mobility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21330)
*Chenhui Pan,Tong Xu,Francesco Cancelliere,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NeSAM是一个神经符号框架，结合可微分的Bekker-Wong土力学模型、学习型地形表示和Transformer残差动力学模型，用于越野车辆在可变形地形上的六自由度运动学动力预测。在仿真和真实平台上分别实现最高30%和29%的预测精度提升，并在闭环导航中降低69.4%的Hausdorff距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野车辆在可变形地形上的运动预测具有挑战性，因为下陷、滑移和牵引力随局部土壤条件变化。现有基于学习的运动学-动力学模型直接从数据近似车辆-地形交互，但未显式表示土壤力学，缺乏物理可解释性。

Method: NeSAM由三部分组成：(1) 可微分的Bekker-Wong土力学模型，用于建模依赖土壤的交互力；(2) 从地形观测中估计物理意义土壤参数的方法，并通过扩展卡尔曼滤波在线更新；(3) Transformer残差动力学模型，用于修正解析预测与观测车辆动力学之间的偏差。系统在Chrono多物理引擎构建的Verti-Bench模拟器及物理Verti-4-Wheeler平台上进行评估。

Result: 相比最强基线，NeSAM在仿真中预测精度提升最高30%，在真实数据上提升29%。与闭环导航控制器集成时，通过在线土壤适应提高了穿越成功率，并将到参考轨迹的Hausdorff距离降低了69.4%，表明轨迹跟踪精度显著改善。

Conclusion: NeSAM通过将物理土壤力学模型与学习残差结合，在保持物理可解释性的同时实现了卓越的长时程运动预测精度，并验证了在线土壤参数自适应对闭环导航性能的实际增益。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of off-road vehicle motion over deformable terrain remains challenging because sinkage, slip, and traction vary with local soil conditions. Existing learning-based kinodynamic models directly approximate vehicle-terrain interactions from data but do not explicitly represent soil mechanics and offer limited physical interpretability. To address these limitations, we present NeSAM, a neuro-symbolic framework that combines differentiable Bekker-Wong terramechanics with learned terrain representations and a Transformer-based residual dynamics model for long-horizon, six degree-of-freedom kinodynamic prediction. The terramechanics component models soil-dependent interaction forces, while the residual model corrects discrepancies between the analytical prediction and the observed vehicle dynamics. NeSAM further estimates physically meaningful soil parameters from terrain observations and updates them online using an extended Kalman filter. We evaluate NeSAM in Verti-Bench, a simulator built on the Chrono multiphysics engine, and validate its performance on a physical Verti-4-Wheeler platform. NeSAM improves prediction accuracy by up to 30% in simulation and 29% on real-world data relative to the strongest compared baselines. When integrated with a close-loop navigation controller, NeSAM further improves traversal success rate through online soil adaptation while reduces Hausdorff distance to the reference trajectory by 69.4%, indicating improved trajectory tracking accuracy.

</details>


### [264] [ViTacPhys: Physical Property-Aware Grasping from Human Visual-Tactile Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21355)
*Yiwen Liu,Yujun Zhu,Kui Jia,Zhao Liao,Yangwei You,Shuaijun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ViTacPhys，一个结合视觉-触觉的框架，从人类操作演示中估计物体质量、摩擦系数类别和连续刚度，并作为在线模块用于自适应抓取，在分布内物体上达到95.0%的总抓取成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的动作模型在复杂操作中表现强大，但很少利用显式物体物理属性来调整策略，缺乏对质量、摩擦系数和刚度等物理特性的感知与利用。

Method: ViTacPhys包含视觉-触觉框架和数据采集系统，结合时间视觉-触觉建模、跨注意力多模态融合及视觉-语言模型语义先验，从60个刚性和可变形物体的人类操作演示中估计物理属性。通过有限机器人遥操作数据、机器人风格视频增强和匹配动作的人类演示，将模型从人类域迁移到机器人域，并部署为自适应抓取的在线模块。

Result: 在已知物体上：质量分类准确率97.2%，摩擦系数分类准确率98.8%，刚度MAPE为5.51%。在已知类别的新物体上：质量准确率87.5%，摩擦系数准确率97.5%，刚度MAPE为9.08%。物理属性条件策略在分布内物体上总抓取成功率为95.0%，分布外物体为83.4%；且抓取力曲线比ACT更接近人类遥操作。

Conclusion: 显式估计和利用物体物理属性对于真实世界自适应抓取是可行且有效的，该框架展示了从人类演示到机器人部署的完整迁移路径。

Abstract: Recent vision-based action models have demonstrated strong capabilities in complex manipulation, but they rarely leverage explicit object physical properties to adapt their policies. We introduce ViTacPhys, a visual-tactile framework and data acquisition system that estimates object mass and friction-coefficient classes, together with continuous stiffness, from human manipulation demonstrations. Trained on data from 60 rigid and deformable objects, ViTacPhys combines temporal visual-tactile modeling, cross-attention multimodal fusion, and a semantic prior derived from a vision-language model. On seen objects, it achieves 97.2% mass classification accuracy, 98.8% friction-coefficient classification accuracy, and a stiffness mean absolute percentage error (MAPE) of 5.51%. On held-out objects from known categories, it achieves 87.5% mass accuracy, 97.5% friction-coefficient accuracy, and a stiffness MAPE of 9.08%. We transfer ViTacPhys from the human domain to the robot domain using limited robot teleoperation data, robot-style video augmentation, and human demonstrations with matched actions, and deploy it as an online module for adaptive grasping. The resulting physical-property-conditioned policy achieves total grasping success rates of 95.0% on in-distribution objects and 83.4% on out-of-distribution objects. For out-of-distribution objects successfully grasped by both methods, its force profiles are more consistent with human teleoperation than those produced by ACT. These results demonstrate the feasibility of explicitly estimating and conditioning on object physical properties for real-world adaptive grasping.

</details>
